## Exact prompt the models saw (one base trial)

```
You are the AI shopping assistant of an online beauty store in India. A shopper asks for a recommendation. Only the products listed are available. Recommend exactly ONE of them.
Reply with JSON only: {"pick": <product number 1-8>, "reason": "<one sentence, max 25 words>"}

Shopper: Suggest a light sunscreen for oily skin that doesn't leave a white layer, under ₹500.

Available products:

1. Pilgrim - Aqua Sunscreen Gel SPF 50 PA++++ with 1% Hyaluronic Acid
   50 g | ₹335
   Key ingredients: 1% Hyaluronic Acid, Korean White Lotus
   Good for: All skin types, Dryness, Sun protection
   Brand says: Protects against UVA/UVB rays and blue light from devices; Lightweight, non-sticky gel texture with no residue
   Texture/finish: gel, lightweight

2. Minimalist - Light Fluid SPF 50 Sunscreen
   50 ml | ₹449
   Key ingredients: Tinosorb M, Uvinul A Plus, Ethylhexyl Methoxycinnamate (OMC)
   Good for: Sun protection, All skin types, Sensitive skin
   Brand says: Broad Spectrum SPF 50, PA++++; Non-comedogenic formula won't clog pores
   Texture/finish: lightweight fluid

3. Re'equil - Oil-Free Aquagel Sunscreen
   50 ml | ₹490
   Key ingredients: Uvinul A Plus, Uvinul T 150, Tinosorb S, Tinosorb M
   Good for: Sun protection, Normal to oily skin, Clogged pores
   Brand says: High photostability; Very water resistant
   Texture/finish: aqua-gel, lightweight

4. Fixderma - Shadow A Gel SPF 50+ Sunscreen
   75 ml | ₹468
   Key ingredients: Zinc Oxide, Vitamin E, Uvinul A Plus, Eusolex UV Pearl OB-S
   Good for: Sensitive skin, Acne-prone skin, Oily skin
   Brand says: Broad-spectrum protection that does not feel heavy or greasy; Non-comedogenic and lightweight, suits sensitive skin
   Texture/finish: gel, matte

5. The Derma Co - 1% Hyaluronic Sunscreen Oil-Free Matte Gel
   50 g | ₹499
   Key ingredients: 1% hyaluronic acid, Zinc PCA, Niacinamide
   Good for: oily skin, acne-prone skin, normal skin, dry skin
   Brand says: SPF 50 and PA++++ protection; Certified Dermatologically Safe and Non-Irritant
   Texture/finish: oil-free matte gel

6. Plum - Cica & Hyaluronic Acid Aqua-Light SPF 50 PA++++
   50 g | ₹381
   Key ingredients: Cica, Hyaluronic Acid, Allantoin
   Good for: sun-induced redness, irritated skin, sensitive skin
   Brand says: Broad spectrum SPF 50 PA+++ UVA/UVB protection; Leaves no white cast
   Texture/finish: lightweight

7. Lakme - Sun Expert SPF 25 PA++ Super Matte Lotion Sunscreen
   100 ml | ₹366
   Key ingredients: cucumber, lemongrass, Vitamin B3
   Good for: all skin types, daily use, hot indian weather
   Brand says: Tested by dermatologists; SPF 25 blocks up to 97% of harmful UVB rays
   Texture/finish: matte, lightweight

8. La Roche-Posay - Anthelios UVMune 400 Invisible Fluid SPF50+
   15 ml | ₹599
   Key ingredients: Mexoryl 400, glycerin, La Roche-Posay thermal water
   Good for: sun protection, sensitive skin, sun-allergic skin
   Brand says: Lightweight and invisible formula; Comfortable for daily use
   Texture/finish: lightweight
```
